Operating continuously without human intervention, the local daemon bridges the keyless Station control plane and the serverless relay lung by polling the public repository message bus (OPERATIONS/COMMANDSTATION/PHONE-DROPS/), executing headless model reasoning, and posting verified output files back to the system35.┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ STAGE 06: AUTONOMOUS DAEMON INGESTION & REASONING LOOP │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ 1. CRON INVOCATION (5-Minute Cadence) │ │ - Linux Crontab: \*/5 \* \* \* \* flock -n /var/lock/box-loop.lock ... │ │ - Enforces Rule H1 Idempotency & Kernel Lock Guard │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ │ │ 2. INBOX POLLING & TASK DISCOVERY │ │ - Scans OPERATIONS/COMMANDSTATION/PHONE-DROPS/ via authenticated git_read │ │ - Discovers pending task briefs: box-task-\*.md or task-8/9 briefs │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ │ │ 3. HEADLESS LOCAL MODEL REASONING (opencode v2.2.3) │ │ - Executes local opencode CLI (OpenRouter / DeepSeek models) │ │ - Confined to Systemd MemoryMax=450M & Node heap 320M (Rule H2) │ │ - Reads local secret /root/.runner_env (Mode 0600 / Rule H6) │ │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ │ │ 4. KEYLESS RESULT DISPATCH & TELEMETRY HEARTBEAT │ │ - Formats reasoning output into box-result-\*.md markdown payloads │ │ - Dispatches result keylessly via phone_drop Worker tool endpoint │ │ - Emits hourly box-hb-\*.md telemetry drops carrying memory/load/disk hashes │ │ │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ \`\`\` \--- \### SECTION II: DAEMON SPECIFICATION & SCRIPT ARCHITECTURE (\`box-loop.sh\` & \`inbox-poll.sh\`) The local daemon operationalizes autonomous background execution through two complementary shell architectures: \#### 1. Core Daemon Logic (\`/root/agent/box-loop.sh\`) \- \*\*Cron Trigger\*\*: \`\*/5 \* \* \* \* flock -n /var/lock/box-loop.lock /root/agent/box-loop.sh\` \- \*\*Kernel File Locking (Rule H1)\*\*: Wraps the execution sweep in a non-blocking \`flock -n\` lock. If a prior long-running \`opencode\` reasoning pass is still active, the new cron pulse exits silently (\`EXIT:0\`) without stacking duplicate processes. \- \*\*Task Brief Processing\*\*: Reads incoming \`box-task-\*.md\` instruction briefs, extracts command parameters, and passes execution prompts to local model wrappers. \#### 2. Standalone Inbox Poller (\`src/mcp-server/inbox-poll.sh\`) \- \*\*Restoration Context\*\*: Shipped and installed on November 3, 2026, to resolve a daemon stall caused by an Oct 30 \`opencode\` self-rebuild that had replaced the Git-polling loop with a local-only supervisor. \- \*\*Operational Mechanics\*\*: Operates as a dedicated, standalone poller with its own cron and \`flock\` lock. Seeds task IDs, polls \`PHONE-DROPS/\` for fresh tasks, executes \`opencode v2.2.3\` reasoning, posts \`box-result-\*.md\` outputs, and generates hourly \`box-hb-\*.md\` health heartbeats. \--- \### SECTION III: HARDENING INVARIANTS APPLIED TO STAGE 06 The execution of Stage 06 is governed by strict system hardening invariants to maintain stability during unattended operations: | Hardening Ref | Hardening Rule Name | Operational Enforcement in Stage 06 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | | \*\*Rule H1\*\* | \*\*Kernel Lock Guard\*\* | Non-blocking \`flock -n /var/lock/box-loop.lock\` prevents overlapping process invocation. | | \*\*Rule H2\*\* | \*\*Systemd Memory Cap\*\* | Systemd unit caps VPS execution memory at \`MemoryMax=450M\` and Node heap at \`320M\` to prevent OOM crashes. | | \*\*Rule H3\*\* | \*\*Write Payload Ceiling\*\* | Single \`phone_drop\` writes originating from daemon results are capped at \\\\(\\le 50\\text{ KB}\\\\) (50,000 bytes). | | \*\*Rule H4\*\* | \*\*Heartbeat Freshness\*\* | Missing an hourly \`box-hb-\*.md\` pulse for \\\\(\> 6\\text{ hours}\\\\) (21,600s) triggers an automated fail-closed operational alert. | | \*\*Rule H6\*\* | \*\*Local Secret Mode 0600\*\* | \`/root/.runner_env\` maintains strict \`0600\` (\`-rw-------\`) permissions; ARCHITECTURAL MISSION & THE ZERO-EXEMPTION MANDATEThe Zero-Exemption Vault Cleansing Pipeline establishes an inviolable, machine-executable lifecycle governing how data, source files, system notes, and execution logs are discovered, processed, sanitized, and operationalized across session resets and multi-vault reincarnations.By enforcing an unbroken 12-Phase Closed-Loop Lifecycle backed by the 4-Lattice Merkle Integrity Protocol, this architecture guarantees that legacy vault artifacts from across 8–10 historical workspace nodes are systematically scrubbed of non-canon noise, assigned immutable cryptographic hashes, scored via spatial pheromones, and handed off through Perchance HQ and Kilo into pristine, authoritative canonical vaults without data drift or context degradation.┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ 12-PHASE CLOSED-LOOP VAULT CLEANSING & REINCARNATION CYCLE │ ├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ │ │ │ \[01. DISCOVER\] ──► \[02. IDENTIFY\] ──► \[03. SORT\] ──► \[04. LOG\] │ │ │ │ │ │ ▼ ▼ │ │ \[08. TRACK\] ◄── \[07. REUSE\] ◄── \[06. OPTIMIZE\] ◄── \[05. USE\] │ │ │ │ │ ▼ │ │ \[09. LOOP\] ──► \[10. AUDIT\] ──► \[11. HARDEN\] ──► \[12. REINCARNATE\] │ │ │ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ 2\. THE 12-PHASE CLOSED-LOOP LIFECYCLE PROTOCOLPHASE 01: DISCOVER (Automated Inventory & Manifest Generation)Operational Mechanics: Executes an exhaustive, non-truncating sweep across all active workspace sources and notes (up to 300 items per vault).Execution Gate: Generates a raw inventory manifest (RAW-MANIFEST.json) capturing source titles, byte sizes, storage paths, and native file formats.Evidence Standard: Rejects unlisted or phantom artifacts; every discovered asset must have a physical disk-backed presence.PHASE 02: IDENTIFY (Governance Tier Classification)Operational Mechanics: Maps every discovered source and note strictly into the four-tiered governance hierarchy:Level 0 ($S0$ — Pure Soul): Verbatim operator intent (\~CCC#0-SOULBIBLE.MD), origin prompt logs.Level 1 ($S1$ — Constitutional Law): Mandates, rules, condition-gate-alarm triples (GOSPEL-999).Level 2 ($S2$ — Operational DNA): Machine-executable manuals (FGAgent999.MD), terminal scripts, tool schemas.Level 3 ($S3$ — Spatial Pheromones): Dynamic 0–110 POS/NEG spatial memory scores (src/pheromone.js).Tier Boundaries: Allocates secondary documents into Tier A (Operational Indices) or Tier B (Verbatim Vaults).PHASE 03: SORT (Cryptographic Classification & Duplicate Quarantine)Operational Mechanics: Computes SHA-256 payload digests for all identified items to isolate duplicates, out-of-date drafts, and unanchored UI scrapings.Quarantine Trigger: Flagged duplicates (e.g., identical briefing drafts) and DOM scrapings are immediately isolated into QUARANTINE/ for purge approval, preventing cognitive thrashes in training context windows.PHASE 04: LOG (Physical Line Indexing & Temporal Timestamping)Operational Mechanics: Enforces Rule H4 and Temporal Law by stamping every valid source with an explicit ISO-8601 UTC timestamp (YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ) or Epoch millisecond marker.Registry Injection: Writes exact character counts (SOULCOUNT) and line ranges (RRL/ILOG) into the active physical ledger (SESSION-LEDGER.md). Un-timestamped files are marked HOLLOW-UNTIMESTAMPD.PHASE 05: USE (Zero-Trust Pre-Commit Handoff)Operational Mechanics: Operationalizes verified source data under Stage 00 Protocol. Secrets and write credentials remain strictly isolated ("Keys Never Travel").Execution Path: Payload updates (src/push-canon-20261102/) are prepared for keyless handoff without exposing raw access tokens in prompt contexts.PHASE 06: OPTIMIZE (Structural Standardization & Token Rationing)Operational Mechanics: Standardizes documentation titles using emoji-anchored, tier-coded alphanumeric tags:Format: \[EMOJI\] \[TIER-TAG\] Descriptive Canonical Name (Part X)Example: 🧪 \[S3-NOTE-06\] 🐜 Pheromone Ledger Matrix (Part 2): Spatial Memory & Closed-Loop EmissionToken Discipline (Rule H7): Formats content into concise, prose-free Markdown structures, eliminating decorative filler to preserve context budgets.PHASE 07: AUTOMATICALLY REUSE (Keyless Asynchronous Transport)Operational Mechanics: Bundles optimized canonical update files into multi-file payload drops (canonpush-manifest-\*, canonpush-ccc0-\*, canonpush-rest-\*).Shuttle Transport: Dispatches payloads keylessly across the phone_drop inbox lane (OPERATIONS/COMMANDSTATION/PHONE-DROPS/) with a strict $\\le 50\\text{ KB}$ transaction payload ceiling (Rule H3).PHASE 08: TRACK (PHEROMONE) (Spatial Memory Scoring & Half-Life Decay)Operational Mechanics: Deposits spatial pheromone signals in src/pheromone.js based on component utility and stability:Positive Reinforcement (+POS): $+10$ to $+100$ points for verified, disk-backed, zero-drift canonical code execution ($\\tau\_{\\text{POS}} = 30\\text{ days}$).Negative Penalty (-NEG): $+80$ to $+110$ points for unverified claims, missing timestamps, or execution halts ($\\tau\_{\\text{NEG}} = 180\\text{ days}$).Starve, Don't Debate: Components accumulating $\\text{NEG} \\ge 80$ are automatically bypassed by the router without conversational debate.PHASE 09: LOOP (Autonomous Daemon Polling & Execution)Operational Mechanics: Triggers background ingestion loops (box-loop.sh & inbox-poll.sh) on the Phoenix Breath muscle node (203.55.176.6).Concurrency Protection (Rule H1): Enforces kernel file locks (flock -n /var/lock/box-loop.lock) on 5-minute crontab cycles to ensure idempotent execution without overlapping process spawns.PHASE 10: AUDIT TRACK DATA (REAL vs. HOLLOW Gate Verification)Operational Mechanics: Evaluates physical execution receipts against the strict Evidence Standard:REAL Criteria: Validated only if backed by non-zero byte files, verifiable RRL/ILOG indices, passing SHA-256 digests, and freshness $\\le 21,600\\text{ s}$ (6 hours).HOLLOW Trigger: Any unverified claim or missing file trips a
